#### 8.10.1 `support_services/errors.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/errors.py
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.errors — 异常族与 HTTP 状态码单一映射（8.1）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+状态码只有四档，路由层不再各处自定义：
+404 不存在 / 403 能力未声明 / 409 状态冲突 / 422 请求或清单非法。
+异常自带 status，service_routes 一次转译（一一对应，不留第二套判定）。
+
+与 manifest.ManifestError 的分工：ManifestError 是解析层异常（ValueError 子类），
+只说明"哪个字段错了"，不携带 HTTP 语义；manager 捕获后统一包成 InvalidRequest。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+
+class ServiceError(Exception):
+    """框架异常基类：status 即 8.1 约定的 HTTP 状态码。"""
+
+    status = 500
+
+    def __init__(self, message: str) -> None:
+        super().__init__(message)
+        self.message = message
+
+
+class ServiceNotFound(ServiceError):
+    status = 404
+
+
+class CapabilityNotDeclared(ServiceError):
+    """清单 capabilities 未声明该动作 / 未对 Agent 开放（8.4 白名单）。"""
+
+    status = 403
+
+
+class StateConflict(ServiceError):
+    """requires_running 未满足 / 生命周期锁被占 / 清单 disabled / 缺 confirm（8.1）。"""
+
+    status = 409
+
+
+class InvalidRequest(ServiceError):
+    """清单字段非法（5.2 schema）或参数值越出定值域。"""
+
+    status = 422
```

#### 8.10.2 `support_services/paths.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/paths.py
@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.paths — 目录锚定与白名单路径解析（5.5）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+三个根各只有一个来源（两态布局同构）：
+- 只读区 ext_service/<id>/      = get_code_root()/ext_service/<id>（开发态=仓库根，发布态=exe 旁）
+- 可写区 <数据区>/services/<id>/ = resolve_db_dir()（与 SQLite 同源解析点，认 OMNIAGENT_DATA_DIR）
+- 事件区 <数据区>/service_events/
+
+resolve_inside() 是清单里所有相对路径的唯一解析入口：解析后必须仍在服务目录内，越界抛
+PathEscapeError。8.4 白名单校验的对象就是这个解析结果，不是 action_id 字符串。
+resolve_executable() 在其上再允许"裸名走 PATH"（9.2.2 的 `powershell` + `-File` 形态）。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import shutil
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Optional
+
+from app.config import get_code_root
+from app.db.database import resolve_db_dir
+
+
+class PathEscapeError(ValueError):
+    """相对路径解析后跑出允许根目录（含 .. 与符号链接穿越）。"""
+
+
+def ext_service_root() -> Path:
+    """只读区根：<代码库根>/ext_service（5.4 扫描入口）。"""
+    return Path(get_code_root()) / "ext_service"
+
+
+def service_dir(service_id: str) -> Path:
+    return ext_service_root() / service_id
+
+
+def data_root() -> Path:
+    """可写数据区根 = SQLite 同源解析点（5.5）。"""
+    return resolve_db_dir()
+
+
+def service_data_dir(service_id: str) -> Path:
+    """服务进程唯一可写区，框架拉起前建好并以 OMNI_SERVICE_DATA 注入（5.5）。"""
+    return data_root() / "services" / service_id
+
+
+def events_dir() -> Path:
+    return data_root() / "service_events"
+
+
+def resolve_inside(base: Path, rel: Optional[str]) -> Path:
+    """把清单里的相对路径解析成实路径，越界即失败。
+
+    绝对路径同样受约束（只允许落在 base 内）：清单是 git 管理的注册契约（5.6），
+    不该把某台机器相关的绝对路径写进去。
+    """
+    base_resolved = base.resolve()
+    raw = Path(rel) if rel else Path(".")
+    target = raw.resolve() if raw.is_absolute() else (base_resolved / raw).resolve()
+    if target != base_resolved and base_resolved not in target.parents:
+        raise PathEscapeError(f"{rel!r} 解析为 {target}，越出 {base_resolved}")
+    return target
+
+
+def ensure_dir(path: Path) -> Path:
+    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    return path
+
+
+def resolve_executable(base: Path, command: str) -> Optional[Path]:
+    """清单 spec.command 的解析：带分隔符的必须在服务目录内，裸名按 PATH 查找（9.2.2）。
+
+    9.2.2 定案 command 可写系统解释器（`powershell` + `-File *.ps1`），故裸名走 PATH；
+    带路径的一律受 resolve_inside 约束，`..` 与绝对路径越界仍然拒绝（8.4 白名单）。
+    解析不到返回 None，交给执行器判"启动失败"——清单现值不存在属于运行期故障，
+    要能走 failed + 退避重试，不能在加载期就判整份清单非法（10.2 第 13 条验收）。
+    """
+    text = (command or "").strip()
+    if not text:
+        return None
+    if "/" in text or "\\" in text:
+        return resolve_inside(base, text)
+    found = shutil.which(text)
+    return Path(found) if found else None
```

#### 8.10.3 `support_services/manifest.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/manifest.py
@@ -0,0 +1,261 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.manifest — 清单 schema 与能力推导（5.2 / 8.7）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+校验失败一律 ManifestError（带"哪个字段错了"），不把 pydantic 原文抛给 UI。
+spec 按 kind 分支建模；kind → capabilities 推导表只允许清单**收窄**（9.2.2：不收窄
+就会出现一个空的"附加操作"标题）。
+
+清单示例带 // 注释（5.2 / 9.2.2 要求可直接落盘），故读取时剥离行注释——只认字符串外的
+//，不认 /* */：URL 里含 "://"，上正则反而更容易写错，这点解析量不值得引库（KISS）。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional, Union
+
+from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, ValidationError, model_validator
+
+from app.support_services.paths import PathEscapeError, resolve_inside
+
+
+class ManifestError(ValueError):
+    """清单非法（5.2 / 5.4），原文进注册表 invalid 项的 error 字段。"""
+
+
+class _Strict(BaseModel):
+    """清单是注册契约：未声明字段一律拒绝（拼错字段名要当场报错，不能静默忽略）。"""
+
+    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
+
+
+class RestartPolicy(_Strict):
+    policy: Literal["on-failure", "never"] = "on-failure"
+    max_attempts: int = Field(3, ge=1, le=10)
+    backoff_seconds: List[int] = Field(default_factory=lambda: [5, 15, 60])
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _backoff_non_empty(self) -> "RestartPolicy":
+        if not self.backoff_seconds:            # 空退避表会让下一次重试立刻发生（自杀循环）
+            self.backoff_seconds = [5]
+        return self
+
+
+class LocalProcessSpec(_Strict):
+    command: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
+    args: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    workdir: str = "."
+    env: Dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    listen_port: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=65535)
+    encoding: Literal["utf-8", "gbk"] = "utf-8"
+    stop: Optional[str] = None
+
+
+class HttpEndpointSpec(_Strict):
+    url: str = ""
+    listen_port: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=65535)
+    encoding: Literal["utf-8", "gbk"] = "utf-8"
+
+
+class HealthSpec(_Strict):
+    check_type: Literal["http", "tcp", "process"] = "process"
+    check_target: str = ""
+    interval_seconds: int = Field(10, ge=2, le=3600)
+    timeout_seconds: int = Field(3, ge=1, le=60)
+    retries: int = Field(2, ge=1, le=10)
+    start_grace_seconds: int = Field(20, ge=0, le=600)
+
+    @model_validator(mode="after")
+    def _target_required(self) -> "HealthSpec":
+        if self.check_type in ("http", "tcp") and not self.check_target.strip():
+            raise ValueError(f"check_type={self.check_type} 必须声明 check_target")
+        return self
+
+
+class ActionSpec(_Strict):
+    id: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,31}$")
+    label: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
+    command: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
+    encoding: Optional[Literal["utf-8", "gbk"]] = None    # 动作级覆盖服务级默认（6.2.5）
+    confirm: bool = False
+    requires_running: bool = True
+    timeout_seconds: int = Field(600, ge=5, le=3600)
+
+
+class LogsSpec(_Strict):
+    kind: Literal["file", "stdout", "none"] = "stdout"
+    path: str = "service.log"
+
+
+class Capabilities(_Strict):
+    start: bool = False
+    stop: bool = False
+    status: bool = True
+    logs: bool = False
+    actions: bool = False
+
+
+class DeclaredCapabilities(_Strict):
+    start: Optional[bool] = None
+    stop: Optional[bool] = None
+    status: Optional[bool] = None
+    logs: Optional[bool] = None
+    actions: Optional[bool] = None
+
+
+# kind → capabilities 推导表（8.7）：清单只能收窄，不能放宽
+_CAPABILITY_MATRIX: Dict[str, Dict[str, bool]] = {
+    "local-process": {"start": True, "stop": True, "status": True, "logs": True, "actions": True},
+    "http-endpoint": {"start": False, "stop": False, "status": True, "logs": False, "actions": True},
+}
+
+# P0 只落两个 kind：mcp-server / plugin 判清单非法，而不是半托管（8.7 备注）
+_KIND_HINT: Dict[str, str] = {
+    "mcp-server": "mcp-server 需宿主拉起与初始化握手，P2 落地",
+    "plugin": "plugin 的启用/禁用语义 P2 落地",
+}
+
+_SPEC_MODELS: Dict[str, Any] = {
+    "local-process": LocalProcessSpec,
+    "http-endpoint": HttpEndpointSpec,
+}
+
+
+class ServiceManifest(_Strict):
+    id: str = Field(..., pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{2,31}$")
+    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
+    description: str = ""
+    kind: str
+    enabled: bool = True
+    activation: Literal["on_demand", "follow_backend", "manual"] = "on_demand"
+    idle_stop_seconds: int = Field(0, ge=0, le=86400)
+    restart: RestartPolicy = Field(default_factory=RestartPolicy)
+    spec: Union[LocalProcessSpec, HttpEndpointSpec]
+    health: HealthSpec = Field(default_factory=HealthSpec)
+    capabilities: Capabilities = Field(default_factory=Capabilities)
+    actions: List[ActionSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
+    logs: LogsSpec = Field(default_factory=LogsSpec)
+
+    @property
+    def listen_port(self) -> Optional[int]:
+        return self.spec.listen_port
+
+    def action(self, action_id: str) -> Optional[ActionSpec]:
+        for candidate in self.actions:
+            if candidate.id == action_id:
+                return candidate
+        return None
+
+
+def _format_validation_error(prefix: str, exc: ValidationError) -> str:
+    parts: List[str] = []
+    for err in exc.errors():
+        location = ".".join(str(item) for item in err.get("loc", ())) or "(根)"
+        parts.append(f"{prefix}.{location}: {err.get('msg', '非法值')}")
+    return "；".join(parts)
+
+
+def _strip_jsonc(text: str) -> str:
+    """剥离字符串外的 // 行注释（字符串内的 "http://" 必须原样保留）。"""
+    out: List[str] = []
+    in_string = False
+    escaped = False
+    index = 0
+    length = len(text)
+    while index < length:
+        char = text[index]
+        if in_string:
+            out.append(char)
+            if escaped:
+                escaped = False
+            elif char == "\\":
+                escaped = True
+            elif char == '"':
+                in_string = False
+        elif char == '"':
+            in_string = True
+            out.append(char)
+        elif char == "/" and index + 1 < length and text[index + 1] == "/":
+            while index < length and text[index] not in "\r\n":
+                index += 1
+            continue
+        else:
+            out.append(char)
+        index += 1
+    return "".join(out)
+
+
+def _merge_capabilities(kind: str, declared: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Capabilities:
+    base = _CAPABILITY_MATRIX[kind]
+    try:
+        wanted = DeclaredCapabilities.model_validate(declared or {})
+    except ValidationError as exc:
+        raise ManifestError(_format_validation_error("capabilities", exc)) from exc
+    values: Dict[str, bool] = {}
+    for field_name in ("start", "stop", "status", "logs", "actions"):
+        override = getattr(wanted, field_name)
+        values[field_name] = base[field_name] if override is None else (base[field_name] and override)
+    return Capabilities(**values)
+
+
+def validate_paths(manifest: ServiceManifest, service_dir: Path) -> None:
+    """白名单校验对象是解析后的实路径（8.4）：工作目录、每个动作脚本都必须在服务目录内。
+
+    裸名命令（`powershell`）不在此判存在性：清单现值写错要在运行期走 failed + 退避（10.2 第 13 条），
+    加载期只拦"带路径却跑出服务目录"这类越界（resolve_executable 在启动时再做一次）。
+    """
+    try:
+        if isinstance(manifest.spec, LocalProcessSpec):
+            if "/" in manifest.spec.command or "\\" in manifest.spec.command:
+                resolve_inside(service_dir, manifest.spec.command)
+            resolve_inside(service_dir, manifest.spec.workdir)
+        for action in manifest.actions:
+            resolve_inside(service_dir, action.command)
+    except (PathEscapeError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
+        raise ManifestError(str(exc)) from exc
+
+
+def parse_manifest(raw: Any, service_dir: Path) -> ServiceManifest:
+    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
+        raise ManifestError("service.json 顶层必须是 JSON 对象")
+    kind = str(raw.get("kind") or "").strip()
+    spec_model = _SPEC_MODELS.get(kind)
+    if spec_model is None:
+        hint = _KIND_HINT.get(kind, f"未知 kind={kind!r}（P0 支持 {sorted(_SPEC_MODELS)}）")
+        raise ManifestError(hint)
+    data = dict(raw)
+    try:
+        data["spec"] = spec_model.model_validate(raw.get("spec") or {})
+    except ValidationError as exc:
+        raise ManifestError(_format_validation_error("spec", exc)) from exc
+    data["capabilities"] = _merge_capabilities(kind, raw.get("capabilities"))
+    if kind == "http-endpoint" and "health" not in raw:
+        url = getattr(data["spec"], "url", "")
+        if not url:
+            raise ManifestError("kind=http-endpoint 必须给 spec.url 或显式 health（监控目标不可推断）")
+        data["health"] = {"check_type": "http", "check_target": url}
+    try:
+        manifest = ServiceManifest.model_validate(data)
+    except ValidationError as exc:
+        raise ManifestError(_format_validation_error("manifest", exc)) from exc
+    validate_paths(manifest, service_dir)
+    return manifest
+
+
+def load_manifest(service_dir: Path) -> ServiceManifest:
+    """读并校验 <service_dir>/service.json；id 必须等于目录名（5.1 目录即服务）。"""
+    path = service_dir / "service.json"
+    try:
+        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
+        raise ManifestError("缺少 service.json") from exc
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise ManifestError(f"读取 service.json 失败: {exc}") from exc
+    try:
+        raw = json.loads(_strip_jsonc(text))
+    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
+        raise ManifestError(f"service.json 非法 JSON（第 {exc.lineno} 行）: {exc.msg}") from exc
+    manifest = parse_manifest(raw, service_dir)
+    if manifest.id != service_dir.name:
+        raise ManifestError(f"id={manifest.id!r} 与目录名 {service_dir.name!r} 不一致（5.1）")
+    return manifest
```

#### 8.10.4 `utils/net_utils.py`

端口三问（在监听吗 / 谁占着 / 何时释放）是通用能力，不是服务框架私有：任何需要"杀掉后确认释放"的子系统都用得上，故落 `app/utils/` 而不是包内。`port_listening` 与 `port_owner_pid` 共用同一次 `net_connections` 枚举（`_listen_conn`），不各扫一遍。

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/utils/net_utils.py
@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+# 编辑历史:
+# 2026-11-13 - 小欧 - 新建: 本机 TCP 监听查询与端口释放确认(psutil, 零子进程), 供支撑服务框架
+#   的"树杀后必须确认端口释放"(8.2 第 5 步)与 unknown 态人工接管取占用者(7.5)共用
+"""net_utils — 本机 TCP 监听查询与释放确认(零子进程) — 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+只回答三件事: 这个端口有没有人在 LISTEN / 占着它的是哪个 pid / 等到什么时候释放。
+不做连接探测(那是 open_connection 的事)、不做 HTTP 判定(那是 probe 的事)。
+psutil.net_connections 是阻塞调用, 调用方一律 asyncio.to_thread(8.6)。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+from typing import Any, Optional
+
+import psutil
+
+from app.logger import logger
+
+PORT_POLL_STEP_SECONDS = 0.2    # 有界轮询步长; 与进程退出的观察节奏各自独立可调
+
+__all__ = ["port_listening", "port_owner_pid", "wait_port_free"]
+
+
+def _listen_conn(port: int) -> Optional[Any]:
+    """该端口上处于 LISTEN 的连接; 枚举失败按"无人监听"处理, 绝不让查询异常打断调用方。"""
+    try:
+        for conn in psutil.net_connections(kind="tcp"):
+            if conn.laddr and conn.laddr.port == port and conn.status == psutil.CONN_LISTEN:
+                return conn
+    except psutil.Error as exc:
+        logger.warning(f"[net] 端口 {port} 枚举失败(按未监听处理): {exc}")
+    return None
+
+
+def port_listening(port: Optional[int]) -> bool:
+    """端口是否有人在 LISTEN(8.2 第 5 步: 树杀后必须确认释放)。"""
+    if not port:
+        return False
+    return _listen_conn(port) is not None
+
+
+def port_owner_pid(port: Optional[int]) -> Optional[int]:
+    """占用该端口的 PID: 给 unknown 态的人工接管与事件 context 现场用(7.5 / 8.9)。"""
+    if not port:
+        return None
+    conn = _listen_conn(port)
+    return conn.pid if conn is not None else None
+
+
+async def wait_port_free(port: Optional[int], timeout: float = 10.0) -> bool:
+    """有界轮询端口释放: 树杀只负责杀, "确认"由本函数做, 绝不无限等(8.2 第 5 步)。"""
+    if not port:
+        return True
+    waited = 0.0
+    while waited <= timeout:
+        if not await asyncio.to_thread(port_listening, port):
+            return True
+        await asyncio.sleep(PORT_POLL_STEP_SECONDS)
+        waited += PORT_POLL_STEP_SECONDS
+    return not await asyncio.to_thread(port_listening, port)
```

#### 8.10.5 `utils/proc_utils.py`（改）

收养来的实例没有 `Popen` 句柄，只能按 PID 问系统；"杀完确认"也需要一个统一的入口。这两件事本就属于进程工具层（仓库已有的 `kill_process_tree` / `kill_pid_descendants` 就在这里），按"层 = 目录"落回 `app/utils/proc_utils.py`，不在服务框架里另造一份。

杀与验仍是独立函数：清理链只调 `kill_process_tree` / `kill_pid_descendants`，绝不在里面塞等待；要确认消失的调用方自己调 `wait_pid_gone`（有界，到点返回 False）。

 #   输出管道(实证孤儿 ping), Windows 父死子不重挂, 仍可按 pid 枚举后代清杀
+# 2026-11-13 - 小欧 - 新增 pid_alive / kill_pid / wait_pid_gone: 支撑服务框架要"杀完确认"与
+#   "按 pid 判活"(收养来的实例没有 Popen 句柄), 与本模块既有的树杀同源(psutil, 零建进程)
 # 2026-11-04 - 小欧 - P20 二次修复: 后代枚举改 ppid 全量映射 + BFS(Process(pid).children() 在根退出后抛
 #   NoSuchProcess, 映射法不依赖根存活) — 小欧-2026-11-04
 """proc_utils — 通用进程工具(零子进程) — 小欧 2026-11-04
@@ -14,9 +16,10 @@
 executor 槽报废, 7 个 pwsh 成孤儿, 清理链一条完成日志都没有)。
 ⇒ 本模块只用 psutil(直接 Win32 API) + `proc.kill()`(TerminateProcess), 全程零建进程。
 
-【只负责"杀", 不负责"确认"】确认死亡由调用方用 `_poll_pid_exit` 有界轮询 —— 杀与验分离,
-避免在清理链里塞进额外等待(SRP)。
+【杀与验分离】清理链只用 kill_process_tree / kill_pid_descendants, 绝不在里面塞等待(SRP);
+需要确认进程真的没了, 用 wait_pid_gone 有界轮询 —— 两者是独立函数, 各自单用, 谁也不调谁。
 """
+import asyncio
 import subprocess
 from typing import Optional
 
@@ -27,7 +30,9 @@ try:                                    # psutil 缺失时降级为"只杀根进
 except ImportError:                     # pragma: no cover - 依赖齐全环境不会走到
     _psutil = None
 
-__all__ = ["kill_process_tree", "kill_pid_descendants"]
+PID_POLL_STEP_SECONDS = 0.2     # 有界轮询步长; 与端口释放的观察节奏各自独立可调
+
+__all__ = ["kill_process_tree", "kill_pid_descendants", "pid_alive", "kill_pid", "wait_pid_gone"]
 
 
 def kill_process_tree(proc: Optional["subprocess.Popen"]) -> None:
@@ -85,3 +90,48 @@ def kill_pid_descendants(root_pid: Optional[int]) -> None:
             _psutil.Process(pid).kill()
         except Exception:
             pass
+
+
+def pid_alive(pid: Optional[int]) -> bool:
+    """该 PID 是否仍存活 — 2026-11-13 小欧
+
+    收养来的实例没有 Popen 句柄, 只能按 pid 问系统。psutil 缺失或查询失败一律按"不存活",
+    让调用方走"重新拉起"这条确定的路, 而不是卡在"也许还活着"。
+    """
+    if not pid or _psutil is None:
+        return False
+    try:
+        return _psutil.Process(pid).is_running()
+    except Exception:
+        return False
+
+
+def kill_pid(pid: Optional[int]) -> None:
+    """按 PID 结束单个进程(TerminateProcess, 零建进程) — 2026-11-13 小欧
+
+    与 kill_pid_descendants 的分工: 这里只杀给定的这一个, 不管它的后代。
+    幂等; 对已死/无权限进程静默(清理路径不允许再抛异常)。
+    """
+    if not pid or _psutil is None:
+        return
+    try:
+        _psutil.Process(pid).kill()
+    except Exception as exc:
+        logger.debug(f"[proc] 结束 pid={pid} 失败(已退出或无权限, 忽略): {exc}")
+
+
+async def wait_pid_gone(pid: Optional[int], timeout: float = 10.0) -> bool:
+    """有界轮询 PID 消失 — 2026-11-13 小欧
+
+    杀与验分离: 清理链不等, 需要确认的调用方用本函数。到点未消失返回 False,
+    由调用方判"停止未确认", 绝不在这里无限等。
+    """
+    if not pid:
+        return True
+    waited = 0.0
+    while waited <= timeout:
+        if not await asyncio.to_thread(pid_alive, pid):
+            return True
+        await asyncio.sleep(PID_POLL_STEP_SECONDS)
+        waited += PID_POLL_STEP_SECONDS
+    return not await asyncio.to_thread(pid_alive, pid)

#### 8.10.6 `utils/win_job_object.py`（改）

8.3 主方案只有两行改动：常量与 `LimitFlags`。设了 `SILENT_BREAKAWAY_OK` 之后，子进程默认落在 Job 之外，`local_process.py` **不得**再传 `subprocess.CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB`（只设 SILENT 位时带该标志会被 `CreateProcess` 拒绝）。

该文件在库内以 CRLF 存储（`git ls-files --eol` 为 `i/crlf`），下面按 LF 记录 diff；落地用 `git apply --ignore-whitespace`，或直接按两处手改（常量 2 行 + `LimitFlags` 4 行）。

#### 8.10.7 `support_services/registry.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/registry.py
@@ -0,0 +1,151 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.registry — ext_service 扫描与不可变快照（5.2 / 5.4）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+差分热扫描规则：清单内容 hash 未变则不动（不打断运行中服务）；变了保留运行态并返回 dirty_ids
+（标 config_dirty）；扫描 IO 异常保留上次快照——8.8 明令禁止"清空注册表让页面全空"。
+读方拿到的是冻结快照，拿不到活字典（8.5 单一写者）。
+ManifestWatcher 是 5.4 热扫描的线程侧：只比对签名并回调，注册表仍由 scan() 单点重写。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hashlib
+import threading
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Callable, Dict, FrozenSet, Optional, Tuple
+
+from app.logger import logger
+from app.utils.time_utils import get_local_iso_timestamp
+
+from app.support_services import paths
+from app.support_services.manifest import ManifestError, ServiceManifest, load_manifest
+
+MANIFEST_FILENAME = "service.json"
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class RegistryItem:
+    """一个服务目录的扫描结果：manifest 为 None 即清单非法（error 给 UI 原文）。"""
+
+    service_id: str
+    service_dir: Path
+    content_hash: str
+    manifest: Optional[ServiceManifest] = None
+    error: Optional[str] = None
+
+    @property
+    def invalid(self) -> bool:
+        return self.manifest is None
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class RegistrySnapshot:
+    items: Dict[str, RegistryItem] = field(default_factory=dict)
+    dirty_ids: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset()
+    scanned_at: str = ""
+
+    def get(self, service_id: str) -> Optional[RegistryItem]:
+        return self.items.get(service_id)
+
+
+def _manifest_hash(path: Path) -> str:
+    return hashlib.sha1(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+
+
+def _empty() -> RegistrySnapshot:
+    return RegistrySnapshot(items={}, dirty_ids=frozenset(), scanned_at=get_local_iso_timestamp())
+
+
+def scan(previous: Optional[RegistrySnapshot] = None) -> RegistrySnapshot:
+    """扫描 ext_service/*/service.json。无清单的子目录不算服务（5.1 目录即服务）。"""
+    root = paths.ext_service_root()
+    if not root.is_dir():
+        logger.warning(f"[svc] ext_service 不存在: {root}（页面将为空，5.5 打包链必检）")
+        return _empty()
+    try:
+        children = sorted(child for child in root.iterdir() if child.is_dir())
+    except OSError as exc:
+        logger.warning(f"[svc] 扫描 {root} 失败，保留上次注册表: {exc}")
+        return previous or _empty()
+    items: Dict[str, RegistryItem] = {}
+    dirty: set = set()
+    for child in children:
+        manifest_path = child / MANIFEST_FILENAME
+        if not manifest_path.is_file():
+            continue
+        service_id = child.name
+        try:
+            content_hash = _manifest_hash(manifest_path)
+        except OSError as exc:
+            content_hash = ""
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] 读取 {manifest_path} 失败: {exc}")
+        try:
+            items[service_id] = RegistryItem(service_id, child, content_hash, load_manifest(child))
+        except ManifestError as exc:
+            items[service_id] = RegistryItem(service_id, child, content_hash, None, str(exc))
+        earliest = previous.get(service_id) if previous is not None else None
+        if earliest is not None and earliest.content_hash != content_hash:
+            dirty.add(service_id)
+    return RegistrySnapshot(items=items, dirty_ids=frozenset(dirty), scanned_at=get_local_iso_timestamp())
+
+
+WATCH_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 2.0     # 看门狗比对间隔（≤20 个目录，一次 scandir + stat 可忽略）
+
+
+class ManifestWatcher:
+    """ext_service 看门狗：只比对签名并回调，不读清单、不改注册表（5.4 / 8.6）。
+
+    用线程轮询而非文件事件：不引入 watchdog 依赖（打包链少一个包），≤20 个目录的
+    scandir + stat 在 2s 周期上可忽略。回调是 manager.request_rescan，变更一律回到
+    事件循环里落地——线程越界改字典是 8.6 明令禁止的那类"读到半截"故障。
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, on_change: Callable[[], None],
+                 interval: float = WATCH_INTERVAL_SECONDS) -> None:
+        self._on_change = on_change
+        self._interval = interval
+        self._stop = threading.Event()
+        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
+        self._signature: Tuple[Tuple[str, int, int], ...] = ()
+
+    def start(self) -> None:
+        if self._thread is not None:
+            return
+        self._signature = self._read_signature()      # 基线：启动时的既有清单不算变更
+        self._thread = threading.Thread(
+            target=self._run, name="svc-manifest-watch", daemon=True)
+        self._thread.start()
+
+    def stop(self) -> None:
+        self._stop.set()
+        thread, self._thread = self._thread, None
+        if thread is not None:
+            thread.join(timeout=2.0)
+
+    def _run(self) -> None:
+        while not self._stop.wait(self._interval):
+            signature = self._read_signature()
+            if signature == self._signature:
+                continue
+            self._signature = signature
+            try:
+                self._on_change()
+            except Exception:              # 回调异常不能杀线程（下一轮变更还要再发）
+                logger.exception("[svc] 清单变更信号投递失败")
+
+    def _read_signature(self) -> Tuple[Tuple[str, int, int], ...]:
+        """(目录名, mtime_ns, size) 排序元组；根目录不可读时沿用上一轮（不误报变更）。"""
+        root = paths.ext_service_root()
+        entries = []
+        try:
+            for child in root.iterdir():
+                if not child.is_dir():
+                    continue
+                try:
+                    stat = (child / MANIFEST_FILENAME).stat()
+                except OSError:
+                    continue
+                entries.append((child.name, int(stat.st_mtime_ns), int(stat.st_size)))
+        except OSError:
+            return self._signature
+        return tuple(sorted(entries))
```

#### 8.10.8 `support_services/state_machine.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/state_machine.py
@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.state_machine — 全状态集与迁移矩阵（7.1）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+唯一写者（8.5）：其它模块只能 submit 迁移请求，不能直接赋值。非法迁移**不抛异常打断引擎**——
+记 warning 保持原状态：一个服务的错状态不能冻结全站（7.1 规则）。
+
+矩阵补一条 `stopped → invalid`：清单在服务没在跑的时候变非法，直接从 stopped 置 invalid。
+7.1 原文只给了 `disabled → invalid`，而"运行中清单变非法不动进程"（8.10 收口 1）要求
+非运行态能直接落到 invalid，否则要为一条清单笔误绕道 disabled。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from typing import Any, Dict, FrozenSet, Optional
+
+from app.logger import logger
+
+STATES: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset({
+    "stopped", "starting", "running", "stopping", "failed", "unknown", "invalid", "disabled",
+})
+
+# 迁移合法性矩阵（7.1 表 + stopped→invalid）：state_machine 的断言来源
+TRANSITIONS: Dict[str, FrozenSet[str]] = {
+    "stopped": frozenset({"starting", "unknown", "disabled", "invalid"}),
+    "starting": frozenset({"running", "failed", "stopping", "disabled"}),
+    "running": frozenset({"failed", "stopping", "unknown", "disabled"}),
+    "stopping": frozenset({"stopped", "failed", "disabled"}),
+    "failed": frozenset({"starting", "stopping", "stopped", "disabled"}),
+    "unknown": frozenset({"running", "stopped", "disabled"}),
+    "invalid": frozenset({"stopped", "disabled"}),
+    "disabled": frozenset({"stopped", "invalid"}),
+}
+
+# 变更钩子：service_id, from, to, reason, trigger, context —— 直接落 8.9 事件信封
+ChangeHook = Any
+
+
+class StateMachine:
+    """单服务状态机。状态迁移带理由与触发者，日志即埋点（8.9）。"""
+
+    def __init__(self, service_id: str, on_change: Optional[ChangeHook] = None) -> None:
+        self._service_id = service_id
+        self._state = "stopped"
+        self._on_change = on_change
+
+    @property
+    def state(self) -> str:
+        return self._state
+
+    def force_initial(self, state: str) -> None:
+        """收养/启动恢复专用：跨进程重启没有"上一次状态"，走矩阵只会刷满非法迁移告警。"""
+        if state not in STATES:
+            raise ValueError(f"未定义状态: {state!r}")
+        self._state = state
+
+    def submit(self, to: str, reason: str, trigger: str = "self-heal",
+               context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> bool:
+        """提交迁移请求；成功 True，非法或原地不动 False（调用方据此决定是否继续）。"""
+        if to not in STATES:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] {self._service_id} 迁移目标未定义 {to!r}，保持 {self._state}")
+            return False
+        source = self._state
+        if to == source:
+            return False
+        if to not in TRANSITIONS[source]:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] {self._service_id} 非法迁移 {source}->{to}（{reason}），保持 {source}")
+            return False
+        self._state = to
+        logger.info(f"[svc] {self._service_id} state {source}->{to} ({reason})")
+        if self._on_change is not None:
+            try:
+                self._on_change(self._service_id, source, to, reason, trigger, context or {})
+            except Exception:            # 事件回调失败不得反过来打断状态迁移（8.8）
+                logger.exception(f"[svc] {self._service_id} 状态变更回调异常（迁移已生效）")
+        return True
```

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/runtime_store.py
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.runtime_store — 运行时快照读写（5.5 / 8.5）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+services_runtime.json 是孤儿收养的唯一数据源，唯一写者是 manager。原子写（临时文件 +
+os.replace）：半截文件会让下次启动把在跑的服务判成 stopped 并重复拉起，同一端口两个实例。
+读失败一律返回空表——8.8 要求"降级 unknown + 告警"，绝不阻断框架启动。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
+
+from app.logger import logger
+
+from app.support_services.paths import data_root
+
+SNAPSHOT_FILENAME = "services_runtime.json"
+SNAPSHOT_VERSION = 1
+
+
+class RuntimeStore:
+    def __init__(self, path: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
+        self._path = path or (data_root() / SNAPSHOT_FILENAME)
+
+    @property
+    def path(self) -> Path:
+        return self._path
+
+    def load(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
+        try:
+            raw = json.loads(self._path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        except FileNotFoundError:
+            return {}
+        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] 运行时快照读失败（按未知处理）: {exc}")
+            return {}
+        services = raw.get("services") if isinstance(raw, dict) else None
+        if not isinstance(services, dict):
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] 运行时快照结构不合法: {self._path}")
+            return {}
+        return {str(key): value for key, value in services.items() if isinstance(value, dict)}
+
+    def save(self, services: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
+        payload = {"version": SNAPSHOT_VERSION, "services": services}
+        temp_path = self._path.with_suffix(self._path.suffix + ".tmp")
+        try:
+            self._path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+            temp_path.write_text(
+                json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+            os.replace(temp_path, self._path)          # 原子替换：读方永远看不到半截 JSON
+        except OSError as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] 运行时快照写失败（降级为无快照运行）: {exc}")
+            try:
+                temp_path.unlink()
+            except OSError:
+                pass
```

#### 8.10.10 `support_services/events.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/events.py
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.events — 事件信封、环形缓冲与落盘（8.9）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+一个信封管四类事件。type / trigger 是定值域（服务侧不许自造第五个值），from / to 只有
+transition 有值，context 记"出事当时的现场"——8.9.1 的故障归因覆盖率只认 context 非空的失败条数。
+落盘走 to_thread（8.6 阻塞 IO 不上事件循环），落盘失败只告警，不影响状态迁移。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import json
+from collections import deque
+from typing import Any, Deque, Dict, List, Optional
+
+from app.logger import logger
+from app.utils.time_utils import get_local_iso_timestamp
+
+from app.support_services import paths
+
+EVENT_TYPES = ("transition", "heal", "idle_stop", "action")
+TRIGGERS = ("user", "self-heal", "backend", "scan", "action")
+
+RING_SIZE = 200                 # 环形缓冲条数（8.9），落盘件不设上限
+
+
+def _running_loop() -> Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop]:
+    try:
+        return asyncio.get_running_loop()
+    except RuntimeError:        # 无循环（模块自检/同步调用）时只留内存记录
+        return None
+
+
+class ServiceEventLog:
+    def __init__(self, ring_size: int = RING_SIZE) -> None:
+        self._ring: Deque[Dict[str, Any]] = deque(maxlen=ring_size)
+
+    def emit(self, service_id: str, event_type: str, reason: str, *,
+             from_state: Optional[str] = None, to_state: Optional[str] = None,
+             trigger: str = "self-heal",
+             context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        if event_type not in EVENT_TYPES:
+            raise ValueError(f"未定义的事件类型: {event_type!r}（值域 {EVENT_TYPES}）")
+        if trigger not in TRIGGERS:
+            raise ValueError(f"未定义的触发者: {trigger!r}（值域 {TRIGGERS}）")
+        record: Dict[str, Any] = {
+            "ts": get_local_iso_timestamp(),
+            "service_id": service_id,
+            "type": event_type,
+            "from": from_state if event_type == "transition" else None,
+            "to": to_state if event_type == "transition" else None,
+            "reason": reason,
+            "trigger": trigger,
+            "context": context or {},
+        }
+        self._ring.append(record)
+        loop = _running_loop()
+        if loop is not None:
+            loop.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(self._append_disk, record))
+        return record
+
+    def tail(self, service_id: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        if service_id is None:
+            return list(self._ring)[-limit:]
+        return [record for record in self._ring if record["service_id"] == service_id][-limit:]
+
+    def _append_disk(self, record: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+        try:
+            paths.ensure_dir(paths.events_dir())
+            target = paths.events_dir() / f"{record['service_id']}.jsonl"
+            with target.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+                handle.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
+        except OSError as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] 事件落盘失败（内存环形仍有记录）: {exc}")
```

#### 8.10.11 `support_services/health.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/health.py
@@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.health — 探测执行与连续计数（7.1 / 8.9）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+只回答"这一轮探测结果是什么、连续几次"，不决定状态：转身 failed 还是继续 starting 由
+manager 依据计数 + 宽限期提交迁移（8.7 铁律：执行器与探测只提供事实，状态机单源）。
+详情字段与 C3/C1 一一对应（7.4 detail.health），consecutive_fail 是 8.9 连续失败计数的出口。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import time
+from collections import deque
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from typing import Any, Deque, Dict, List, Optional
+
+from app.logger import logger
+from app.utils.time_utils import get_local_iso_timestamp
+
+from app.support_services.executors.base import Executor, ProbeResult, ServiceContext
+
+HISTORY_SIZE = 30               # C3 点阵环形尾部（7.4）
+
+
+@dataclass
+class ServiceHealth:
+    last_probe_at: Optional[str] = None
+    last_latency_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    consecutive_ok: int = 0
+    consecutive_fail: int = 0
+    last_error: Optional[str] = None
+    history: Deque[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=lambda: deque(maxlen=HISTORY_SIZE))
+
+    def as_detail(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "last_probe_at": self.last_probe_at,
+            "latency_ms": self.last_latency_ms,
+            "consecutive_ok": self.consecutive_ok,
+            "consecutive_fail": self.consecutive_fail,
+            "last_error": self.last_error,
+        }
+
+    def history_list(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        return list(self.history)
+
+
+class HealthProber:
+    async def probe(self, ctx: ServiceContext, executor: Executor, status: ServiceHealth) -> bool:
+        """执行一次探测并更新计数；探测异常计入失败，绝不让健康循环整体退出（8.8）。"""
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        try:
+            result = await executor.probe(ctx)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] {ctx.manifest.id} 探测异常: {exc}")
+            result = ProbeResult(ok=False, error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
+        elapsed_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)
+        status.last_probe_at = get_local_iso_timestamp()
+        status.last_latency_ms = result.latency_ms if result.latency_ms is not None else elapsed_ms
+        status.history.append({
+            "at": status.last_probe_at,
+            "ok": result.ok,
+            "latency_ms": status.last_latency_ms,
+        })
+        if result.ok:
+            status.consecutive_ok += 1
+            status.consecutive_fail = 0
+            status.last_error = None
+        else:
+            status.consecutive_ok = 0
+            status.consecutive_fail += 1
+            status.last_error = result.error or "探测未通过"
+        return result.ok
```
