#### 8.10.13 `support_services/executors/base.py`

契约只留契约。进程/端口原子操作已下沉 `app/utils/proc_utils.py` 与 `app/utils/net_utils.py`，三通道探测独立成 `executors/probe.py`；`build_service_env` / `write_presence` 留在这里，因为它们是 `ServiceContext` 的投影（把上下文摊成子进程能读的东西），不是通用工具。

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/executors/base.py
@@ -0,0 +1,88 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.executors.base — 执行器契约与上下文注入（8.7）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+铁律：执行器不改 state、不写 runtime 快照、不做重试——只回答"事实上发生了什么"。
+本模块只放三样东西：执行器契约（ISP 最小面）、ServiceContext、它的两个投影（进程环境变量
+与 presence 文件）。OS 原子操作归 app/utils/proc_utils.py 与 app/utils/net_utils.py，
+三通道探测归 executors/probe.py——契约与实现不混在一个文件。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, ClassVar, Dict, Optional, Protocol
+
+from app.logger import logger
+
+from app.support_services.manifest import LocalProcessSpec, ServiceManifest
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class ServiceContext:
+    """一次操作所需的全部事实：清单、只读目录、可写目录、当前 PID、在场态。"""
+
+    manifest: ServiceManifest
+    service_dir: Path
+    data_dir: Path
+    pid: Optional[int] = None
+    presence: str = "away"
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class StartResult:
+    ok: bool
+    pid: Optional[int] = None
+    error: Optional[str] = None
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class StopResult:
+    pid_gone: bool
+    port_free: bool
+    error: Optional[str] = None
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class ProbeResult:
+    ok: bool
+    latency_ms: Optional[int] = None
+    error: Optional[str] = None
+
+
+class Executor(Protocol):
+    """ISP 最小面（8.7）：执行器只需要四件事。"""
+
+    kind: ClassVar[str]
+
+    async def start(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> StartResult: ...
+
+    async def stop(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> StopResult: ...
+
+    def snapshot(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> Dict[str, Any]: ...
+
+    async def probe(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> ProbeResult: ...
+
+
+def build_service_env(ctx: ServiceContext) -> Dict[str, str]:
+    """6.7 输入标准的注入全集（5.5 定案）：ID / DATA / DIR / PORT / PRESENCE + 清单 env。"""
+    env = dict(os.environ)
+    env["OMNI_SERVICE_ID"] = ctx.manifest.id
+    env["OMNI_SERVICE_DATA"] = str(ctx.data_dir)
+    env["OMNI_SERVICE_DIR"] = str(ctx.service_dir)
+    env["OMNI_PRESENCE"] = ctx.presence
+    if ctx.manifest.listen_port:
+        env["OMNI_SERVICE_PORT"] = str(ctx.manifest.listen_port)
+    spec = ctx.manifest.spec
+    if isinstance(spec, LocalProcessSpec):
+        env.update(spec.env or {})
+    return env
+
+
+def write_presence(data_dir: Path, presence: str) -> None:
+    """6.7 第 5 条：presence 文件承载运行期变化（环境变量只是启动初值）。"""
+    try:
+        data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        (data_dir / "presence").write_text(presence, encoding="utf-8")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        logger.warning(f"[svc] presence 文件写入失败 {data_dir}: {exc}")
```

#### 8.10.14 `support_services/executors/probe.py`

探测实现从 `base.py` 拆出来的理由：`process` / `tcp` / `http` 三种通道的差别只在这一个函数，执行器只管把 `ServiceContext` 交进来；留在契约文件里会让"契约"与"某一种实现"同层混读。

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/executors/probe.py
@@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.executors.probe — 三通道探测（5.2 health.check_type）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+process 只看登记 PID、tcp 连端口、http 断言 2xx。三种通道的差别只在这一个函数里，
+执行器只管把 ctx 交进来——探测实现不散落进各执行器，也不和契约混在同一模块。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+from typing import Optional, Tuple
+
+import httpx
+
+from app.utils.proc_utils import pid_alive
+
+from app.support_services.executors.base import ProbeResult
+from app.support_services.manifest import HealthSpec
+
+
+def _split_tcp_target(target: str) -> Tuple[str, int]:
+    host, _, port_text = target.rpartition(":")
+    return (host or "127.0.0.1"), int(port_text)
+
+
+async def run_probe(health: HealthSpec, pid: Optional[int]) -> ProbeResult:
+    """三通道探测（5.2 health.check_type）：process 只看进程、tcp 连端口、http 断言 2xx。"""
+    if health.check_type == "process":
+        if pid is None:
+            return ProbeResult(ok=False, error="无登记 PID（快照丢失或未启动）")
+        alive = await asyncio.to_thread(pid_alive, pid)
+        return ProbeResult(ok=alive, error=None if alive else f"进程 {pid} 已退出")
+    if health.check_type == "tcp":
+        try:
+            host, port = _split_tcp_target(health.check_target)
+        except ValueError:
+            return ProbeResult(ok=False, error=f"check_target 非法: {health.check_target!r}")
+        try:
+            _reader, writer = await asyncio.wait_for(
+                asyncio.open_connection(host, port), timeout=health.timeout_seconds)
+            writer.close()
+            return ProbeResult(ok=True)
+        except (OSError, asyncio.TimeoutError) as exc:
+            return ProbeResult(ok=False, error=f"tcp {host}:{port} 连接失败: {exc or '超时'}")
+    try:
+        async with httpx.AsyncClient(
+                timeout=health.timeout_seconds, follow_redirects=False) as client:
+            response = await client.get(health.check_target)
+    except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
+        return ProbeResult(ok=False, error=f"GET {health.check_target} 失败: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
+    if 200 <= response.status_code < 300:
+        return ProbeResult(ok=True)
+    return ProbeResult(ok=False, error=f"GET {health.check_target} 返回 {response.status_code}")
```

#### 8.10.15 `support_services/executors/local_process.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/executors/local_process.py
@@ -0,0 +1,148 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.executors.local_process — 本机进程型执行器（8.2）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+start 只做"把进程拉起来并登记 PID"，不判健康、不写快照、不重试（8.7）。
+stdout 由框架直接落 <OMNI_SERVICE_DATA>/service.log（logs.kind=stdout 的 T1 承诺）：用文件
+句柄直传而不是读线程代收——零读线程、零阻塞，服务写多快都拖不住事件循环。
+停止是验证式（8.2）：可选优雅 POST /shutdown → psutil 树杀 → 确认 PID 消失 → 确认端口释放。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import subprocess
+from typing import IO, Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple
+
+import httpx
+
+from app.logger import logger
+from app.utils.net_utils import wait_port_free
+from app.utils.proc_utils import kill_pid, kill_pid_descendants, kill_process_tree, wait_pid_gone
+
+from app.support_services.executors.base import (
+    ProbeResult,
+    ServiceContext,
+    StartResult,
+    StopResult,
+    build_service_env,
+)
+from app.support_services.executors.probe import run_probe
+from app.support_services.manifest import LocalProcessSpec
+from app.support_services.paths import (
+    PathEscapeError,
+    ensure_dir,
+    resolve_executable,
+    resolve_inside,
+)
+
+GRACEFUL_STOP_TIMEOUT = 5.0
+STOP_CONFIRM_TIMEOUT = 10.0
+
+
+class LocalProcessExecutor:
+    kind = "local-process"
+
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self._popen: Dict[str, subprocess.Popen] = {}
+        self._log_handles: Dict[str, IO[bytes]] = {}
+
+    async def start(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> StartResult:
+        spec = ctx.manifest.spec
+        if not isinstance(spec, LocalProcessSpec):
+            return StartResult(ok=False, error="spec 与 kind=local-process 不匹配")
+        try:
+            executable = resolve_executable(ctx.service_dir, spec.command)
+            workdir = resolve_inside(ctx.service_dir, spec.workdir)
+        except PathEscapeError as exc:
+            return StartResult(ok=False, error=str(exc))
+        if executable is None or not executable.is_file():
+            return StartResult(ok=False, error=f"命令不存在或不可执行: {spec.command}")
+        if not workdir.is_dir():
+            return StartResult(ok=False, error=f"workdir 不存在: {workdir}")
+        try:
+            await asyncio.to_thread(ensure_dir, ctx.data_dir)     # 5.5：可写区框架先建
+        except OSError as exc:
+            return StartResult(ok=False, error=f"数据区创建失败: {exc}")
+        stdout_target, log_handle = self._open_stdout(ctx)
+        try:
+            popen = await asyncio.to_thread(
+                subprocess.Popen,
+                [str(executable)] + list(spec.args),
+                cwd=str(workdir),
+                env=build_service_env(ctx),
+                stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                stdout=stdout_target,
+                stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW | subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP,
+            )
+        except OSError as exc:
+            if log_handle is not None:
+                log_handle.close()
+            return StartResult(ok=False, error=f"Popen 失败: {exc}")
+        self._popen[ctx.manifest.id] = popen
+        if log_handle is not None:
+            self._log_handles[ctx.manifest.id] = log_handle
+        logger.info(f"[svc] {ctx.manifest.id} 进程已拉起 pid={popen.pid} cwd={workdir}")
+        return StartResult(ok=True, pid=popen.pid)
+
+    async def stop(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> StopResult:
+        spec = ctx.manifest.spec
+        popen = self._popen.pop(ctx.manifest.id, None)
+        pid = ctx.pid or (popen.pid if popen is not None else None)
+        graceful_error: Optional[str] = None
+        if popen is not None and getattr(spec, "stop", None):
+            graceful_error = await self._graceful_stop(str(spec.stop))
+        if popen is not None:
+            await asyncio.to_thread(kill_process_tree, popen)     # psutil BFS 树杀，唯一入口
+        elif pid:
+            await asyncio.to_thread(kill_pid_descendants, pid)
+            await asyncio.to_thread(kill_pid, pid)
+        pid_gone = await wait_pid_gone(pid, STOP_CONFIRM_TIMEOUT)
+        port_free = await wait_port_free(getattr(spec, "listen_port", None), STOP_CONFIRM_TIMEOUT)
+        self._close_log(ctx.manifest.id)
+        if not (pid_gone and port_free):
+            logger.warning(
+                f"[svc] {ctx.manifest.id} 停止未完全确认 pid_gone={pid_gone} port_free={port_free}")
+        return StopResult(pid_gone=pid_gone, port_free=port_free, error=graceful_error)
+
+    def snapshot(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        popen = self._popen.get(ctx.manifest.id)
+        if popen is None:
+            return {"pid": ctx.pid, "alive": None}    # 收养来的进程：存活由探测回答，不由快照猜
+        return {"pid": popen.pid, "alive": popen.poll() is None}
+
+    async def probe(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> ProbeResult:
+        pid = ctx.pid
+        if pid is None:
+            popen = self._popen.get(ctx.manifest.id)
+            pid = popen.pid if popen is not None else None
+        return await run_probe(ctx.manifest.health, pid)
+
+    def _open_stdout(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> Tuple[Any, Optional[IO[bytes]]]:
+        logs = ctx.manifest.logs
+        if logs.kind != "stdout":
+            return subprocess.DEVNULL, None            # file / none：服务自写，框架只读
+        path = ctx.data_dir / logs.path
+        try:
+            ensure_dir(path.parent)
+            handle = open(path, "ab")
+        except OSError as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] {ctx.manifest.id} 日志打开失败，stdout 丢弃: {exc}")
+            return subprocess.DEVNULL, None
+        return handle, handle
+
+    def _close_log(self, service_id: str) -> None:
+        handle = self._log_handles.pop(service_id, None)
+        if handle is not None:
+            try:
+                handle.close()
+            except OSError:
+                pass
+
+    async def _graceful_stop(self, url: str) -> Optional[str]:
+        """契约 6.5：声明了 spec.stop 就先给服务一次优雅收场，失败不阻断树杀。"""
+        try:
+            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=GRACEFUL_STOP_TIMEOUT) as client:
+                await client.post(url)
+            return None
+        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
+            return f"优雅停止未成功（转树杀）: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
```

#### 8.10.16 `support_services/executors/http_endpoint.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/executors/http_endpoint.py
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.executors.http_endpoint — 纯监控执行器（8.7）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+不拥有进程：start 恒失败、stop 恒"已停"。清单 capabilities 已按 kind 推导为
+start/stop=false（4.4 能力闸门），UI 不渲染启停按钮；本执行器只把"外部端点活着吗"
+变成一个能被状态机消费的事实——这是"监控别人的服务"与"托管自己的进程"的分界。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from typing import Any, Dict
+
+from app.support_services.executors.base import (
+    ProbeResult,
+    ServiceContext,
+    StartResult,
+    StopResult,
+)
+from app.support_services.executors.probe import run_probe
+from app.support_services.manifest import HttpEndpointSpec
+
+
+class HttpEndpointExecutor:
+    kind = "http-endpoint"
+
+    async def start(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> StartResult:
+        return StartResult(ok=False, error="kind=http-endpoint 不拥有进程（8.7 能力表无 start）")
+
+    async def stop(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> StopResult:
+        return StopResult(pid_gone=True, port_free=True)
+
+    def snapshot(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        return {"pid": None, "alive": None}
+
+    async def probe(self, ctx: ServiceContext) -> ProbeResult:
+        if not isinstance(ctx.manifest.spec, HttpEndpointSpec):
+            return ProbeResult(ok=False, error="spec 与 kind=http-endpoint 不匹配")
+        return await run_probe(ctx.manifest.health, None)
```

#### 8.10.17 `support_services/actions.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/actions.py
@@ -0,0 +1,159 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.actions — 动作白名单执行与任务表（8.4）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+白名单校验对象是 actions[].command 解析后的实路径（清单加载时已过 paths.resolve_inside），
+不是 action_id 字符串——只校验 id 等于给了任意命令执行面。
+执行一律 asyncio.to_thread 卸载：同步长任务跑在事件循环里会僵死全服务（shell_engine 事故铁律）。
+短/长按清单 timeout_seconds 判定（>30s 走任务表），框架不凭 id 猜快慢。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import json
+import subprocess
+import uuid
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
+
+from app.logger import logger
+from app.utils.proc_utils import kill_process_tree
+from app.utils.time_utils import get_local_iso_timestamp
+
+from app.support_services.errors import StateConflict, ServiceNotFound
+from app.support_services.executors.base import ServiceContext
+from app.support_services.manifest import ActionSpec, LocalProcessSpec
+from app.support_services.paths import ensure_dir, resolve_inside
+
+SHORT_ACTION_SECONDS = 30       # 8.4 阈值：超过即走任务表
+TASKS_PER_SERVICE = 50          # 内存任务表上限（落盘件不删，排障要现场）
+
+
+@dataclass
+class ActionTask:
+    task_id: str
+    service_id: str
+    action_id: str
+    state: str = "running"          # running | succeeded | failed | timeout
+    started_at: str = ""
+    finished_at: Optional[str] = None
+    exit_code: Optional[int] = None
+    output: str = ""
+    error: Optional[str] = None
+
+    def as_dict(self, with_output: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
+            "task_id": self.task_id,
+            "service_id": self.service_id,
+            "action_id": self.action_id,
+            "state": self.state,
+            "started_at": self.started_at,
+            "finished_at": self.finished_at,
+            "exit_code": self.exit_code,
+            "error": self.error,
+        }
+        if with_output:
+            payload["output"] = self.output
+        return payload
+
+
+def _wrap_script(command_path) -> List[str]:
+    """CreateProcess 不能直接执行 .bat/.cmd/.ps1，按后缀补宿主解释器（契约 6.3 的真实形态）。"""
+    suffix = command_path.suffix.lower()
+    if suffix in (".bat", ".cmd"):
+        return ["cmd.exe", "/c", str(command_path)]
+    if suffix == ".ps1":
+        return ["powershell.exe", "-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-File", str(command_path)]
+    return [str(command_path)]
+
+
+class ActionRunner:
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self._tasks: Dict[str, ActionTask] = {}
+        self._locks: Dict[str, asyncio.Lock] = {}
+
+    def task(self, task_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        task = self._tasks.get(task_id)
+        if task is None:
+            raise ServiceNotFound(f"动作任务不存在: {task_id}")
+        return task.as_dict()
+
+    async def run(self, ctx: ServiceContext, action: ActionSpec) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        lock = self._locks.setdefault(f"{ctx.manifest.id}:{action.id}", asyncio.Lock())
+        if lock.locked():
+            raise StateConflict(f"动作 {action.id} 正在执行（同类动作串行，7.2 第 6 条）")
+        task = ActionTask(
+            task_id=uuid.uuid4().hex[:12],
+            service_id=ctx.manifest.id,
+            action_id=action.id,
+            started_at=get_local_iso_timestamp(),
+        )
+        self._tasks[task.task_id] = task
+        self._prune(ctx.manifest.id)
+        if action.timeout_seconds <= SHORT_ACTION_SECONDS:
+            await self._execute_locked(lock, ctx, action, task)
+            return task.as_dict()
+        asyncio.create_task(self._execute_locked(lock, ctx, action, task))
+        return task.as_dict()
+
+    async def _execute_locked(self, lock: asyncio.Lock, ctx: ServiceContext,
+                              action: ActionSpec, task: ActionTask) -> None:
+        async with lock:
+            try:
+                exit_code, output = await asyncio.to_thread(self._run_blocking, ctx, action)
+                task.exit_code = exit_code
+                task.output = output
+                task.state = "succeeded" if exit_code == 0 else "failed"
+                if exit_code != 0:
+                    task.error = f"退出码 {exit_code}（契约 6.3：非 0 即失败）"
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                task.state = "timeout"
+                task.error = f"超时 {action.timeout_seconds}s，已树杀动作进程"
+            except Exception as exc:            # 动作失败不影响服务 running 态（8.4）
+                task.state = "failed"
+                task.error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
+            finally:
+                task.finished_at = get_local_iso_timestamp()
+                logger.info(
+                    f"[svc] {task.service_id} action {task.action_id} {task.task_id} {task.state}")
+                await asyncio.to_thread(self._persist, ctx, task)
+
+    def _run_blocking(self, ctx: ServiceContext, action: ActionSpec) -> Tuple[int, str]:
+        spec = ctx.manifest.spec
+        command_path = resolve_inside(ctx.service_dir, action.command)
+        if not command_path.is_file():
+            raise FileNotFoundError(f"动作脚本不存在: {command_path}")
+        workdir = ctx.service_dir
+        if isinstance(spec, LocalProcessSpec):
+            workdir = resolve_inside(ctx.service_dir, spec.workdir)
+        encoding = action.encoding or getattr(spec, "encoding", "utf-8")
+        popen = subprocess.Popen(
+            _wrap_script(command_path),
+            cwd=str(workdir),
+            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
+            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+            creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW,
+        )
+        try:
+            raw, _ = popen.communicate(timeout=action.timeout_seconds)
+        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+            kill_process_tree(popen)        # 契约 6.2.1 框架侧兜底：交互挂起不许无限等
+            popen.communicate(timeout=5)
+            raise
+        exit_code = popen.returncode if popen.returncode is not None else -1
+        return exit_code, raw.decode(encoding, errors="replace")
+
+    def _persist(self, ctx: ServiceContext, task: ActionTask) -> None:
+        try:
+            target_dir = ensure_dir(ctx.data_dir / "actions")
+            (target_dir / f"{task.task_id}.json").write_text(
+                json.dumps(task.as_dict(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        except OSError as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] 动作任务落盘失败（内存表仍有记录）: {exc}")
+
+    def _prune(self, service_id: str) -> None:
+        owned = [task for task in self._tasks.values() if task.service_id == service_id]
+        if len(owned) <= TASKS_PER_SERVICE:
+            return
+        for task in owned[: len(owned) - TASKS_PER_SERVICE]:
+            self._tasks.pop(task.task_id, None)
```

#### 8.10.18 `support_services/manager.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/manager.py
@@ -0,0 +1,849 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services.manager — 生命周期编排（状态机 · 期望态 · 自愈 · 孤儿收养）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+单一写者（8.5）：state 只由 StateMachine 写；desired / heal_paused / heal_attempts /
+last_active_at 只由本类写；runtime 快照只由本类落盘。所有阻塞调用一律 asyncio.to_thread（8.6）。
+
+纠偏循环（7.6）与健康探测同一个 5s tick：desired 与 state 不一致且允许纠偏时，走与按钮完全
+相同的 start 路径，不新增状态、不走捷径。presence 只读外部上报，框架不改它。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import time
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from datetime import datetime
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional
+
+import psutil
+
+from app.logger import logger
+from app.utils.net_utils import port_listening, port_owner_pid, wait_port_free
+from app.utils.proc_utils import kill_pid, kill_pid_descendants, pid_alive, wait_pid_gone
+from app.utils.time_utils import get_local_iso_timestamp
+
+from app.support_services import paths
+from app.support_services.actions import ActionRunner
+from app.support_services.errors import (
+    CapabilityNotDeclared,
+    InvalidRequest,
+    ServiceNotFound,
+    StateConflict,
+)
+from app.support_services.events import ServiceEventLog
+from app.support_services.executors.base import Executor, ServiceContext, write_presence
+from app.support_services.executors.http_endpoint import HttpEndpointExecutor
+from app.support_services.executors.local_process import LocalProcessExecutor
+from app.support_services.health import HealthProber, ServiceHealth
+from app.support_services.manifest import ServiceManifest
+from app.support_services.registry import ManifestWatcher, RegistryItem, RegistrySnapshot, scan
+from app.support_services.runtime_store import RuntimeStore
+from app.support_services.state_machine import StateMachine
+
+TICK_SECONDS = 5.0                    # 健康探测与纠偏同一循环（7.6，不另起线程）
+HEALTHY_RESET_SECONDS = 60.0          # 成功 running 累计该秒数才清零 heal_attempts（7.6 止损）
+ENSURE_POLL_SECONDS = 1.0
+LOG_TAIL_BYTES = 64 * 1024            # 日志尾部只读末 64KB，不整文件加载
+MONITOR_KINDS = ("http-endpoint",)    # 不拥有进程、只观测的 kind（8.7）
+
+
+@dataclass
+class ServiceRuntime:
+    """一个服务的运行期全部可变状态；生命周期锁也在里面（7.2 第 6 条）。"""
+
+    manifest: ServiceManifest
+    service_dir: Path
+    sm: StateMachine
+    health: ServiceHealth = field(default_factory=ServiceHealth)
+    desired: str = "stopped"
+    heal_paused: bool = False
+    heal_attempts: int = 0
+    last_active_at: Optional[str] = None
+    started_monotonic: Optional[float] = None
+    started_at_iso: Optional[str] = None
+    running_since: Optional[float] = None
+    pid: Optional[int] = None
+    last_error: Optional[str] = None
+    config_dirty: bool = False
+    presence_written: str = ""
+    next_probe_at: float = 0.0
+    next_attempt_at: float = 0.0
+    lock: asyncio.Lock = field(default_factory=asyncio.Lock)
+
+
+class ServiceManager:
+    def __init__(self, presence_provider: Optional[Callable[[], str]] = None,
+                 inflight_provider: Optional[Callable[[], int]] = None) -> None:
+        self._registry: RegistrySnapshot = RegistrySnapshot()
+        self._runtimes: Dict[str, ServiceRuntime] = {}
+        self._scan_lock = asyncio.Lock()
+        self._events = ServiceEventLog()
+        self._health = HealthProber()
+        self._actions = ActionRunner()
+        self._runtime_store = RuntimeStore()
+        self._executors: Dict[str, Executor] = {
+            executor.kind: executor
+            for executor in (LocalProcessExecutor(), HttpEndpointExecutor())
+        }
+        self._presence_provider = presence_provider
+        self._inflight_provider = inflight_provider
+        self._loop: Optional[asyncio.AbstractEventLoop] = None
+        self._tick_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
+        self._watcher: Optional[ManifestWatcher] = None
+        self._started = False
+
+    # ── 生命周期 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
+    async def startup(self) -> None:
+        if self._started:
+            return
+        self._started = True
+        self._loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
+        self._watcher = ManifestWatcher(self.request_rescan)
+        self._watcher.start()                # 先起看门狗再扫描：扫描期间的变更也不会漏
+        await self.rescan("backend")
+        await self._restore_from_snapshot()
+        for runtime in list(self._runtimes.values()):
+            manifest = runtime.manifest
+            if (manifest.activation == "follow_backend" and manifest.enabled
+                    and runtime.desired == "running"):
+                asyncio.create_task(self._boot_start(manifest.id))
+        self._tick_task = asyncio.create_task(self._tick_loop())
+        logger.info(f"[svc] 框架已启动: {len(self._runtimes)} 个服务，tick={TICK_SECONDS}s")
+
+    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
+        """只停循环与落盘，**不杀服务进程**——服务必须活得比 backend 久（8.3）。"""
+        if self._tick_task is not None:
+            self._tick_task.cancel()
+            try:
+                await self._tick_task
+            except asyncio.CancelledError:
+                pass
+            self._tick_task = None
+        if self._watcher is not None:
+            self._watcher.stop()
+            self._watcher = None
+        await self._persist()
+        logger.info("[svc] 框架已停机（服务进程保持运行）")
+
+    async def _boot_start(self, service_id: str) -> None:
+        try:
+            await self.start(service_id, trigger="backend")
+        except Exception as exc:
+            logger.warning(f"[svc] {service_id} follow_backend 拉起失败: {exc}")
+
+    def request_rescan(self) -> None:
+        """watchdog 线程唯一交接入口（8.6）：线程禁止直接改注册表，只能投递到循环。"""
+        loop = self._loop
+        if loop is None or loop.is_closed():
+            return
+        loop.call_soon_threadsafe(lambda: asyncio.create_task(self.rescan("scan")))
+
+    async def rescan(self, trigger: str = "scan") -> None:
+        async with self._scan_lock:
+            previous = self._registry
+            snapshot = await asyncio.to_thread(scan, previous)
+            self._registry = snapshot
+            for service_id in list(self._runtimes):
+                if service_id not in snapshot.items:
+                    await self._retire(self._runtimes.pop(service_id), trigger)
+            for service_id, item in snapshot.items.items():
+                if item.manifest is None:
+                    runtime = self._runtimes.get(service_id)
+                    if runtime is not None:
+                        # 8.10 收口 1：运行中的服务只标错误不动进程；没在跑的才置 invalid
+                        runtime.last_error = item.error
+                        if runtime.sm.state in ("stopped", "disabled"):
+                            runtime.sm.submit("invalid", item.error or "清单非法", trigger=trigger)
+                    continue
+                runtime = self._runtimes.get(service_id)
+                if runtime is None:
+                    self._runtimes[service_id] = self._new_runtime(item)
+                    continue
+                runtime.manifest = item.manifest
+                runtime.service_dir = item.service_dir
+                runtime.config_dirty = service_id in snapshot.dirty_ids
+                if runtime.sm.state in ("invalid", "disabled"):
+                    runtime.last_error = None
+                    runtime.sm.submit("stopped", "清单已修正或已启用", trigger=trigger)
+                if not item.manifest.enabled:
+                    await self._disable(runtime)
+
+    async def _retire(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, trigger: str) -> None:
+        """目录被删：先停再摘（5.4），不留无人管理的进程。"""
+        if runtime.sm.state in ("running", "starting", "stopping"):
+            try:
+                async with runtime.lock:
+                    await self._do_stop(runtime, trigger)
+            except Exception:
+                logger.exception(f"[svc] {runtime.manifest.id} 注销前停止失败")
+        self._events.emit(runtime.manifest.id, "transition", "目录移除，服务注销",
+                          from_state=runtime.sm.state, to_state="stopped", trigger=trigger)
+
+    async def _disable(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        if runtime.sm.state in ("running", "starting"):
+            async with runtime.lock:
+                await self._do_stop(runtime, "scan")
+        runtime.desired = "stopped"
+        runtime.sm.submit("disabled", "清单 enabled=false", trigger="scan")
+
+    # ── 人工操作（8.1）────────────────────────────────────────
+    async def start(self, service_id: str, trigger: str = "user") -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        self._require_manageable(runtime)
+        self._require_capability(runtime, "start")
+        runtime.desired = "running"
+        runtime.heal_paused = False
+        self._touch(runtime)
+        await self._start_locked(runtime, trigger)
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def stop(self, service_id: str, trigger: str = "user") -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        self._require_manageable(runtime)
+        self._require_capability(runtime, "stop")
+        if runtime.lock.locked():
+            raise StateConflict("生命周期操作进行中（另一 start/stop/restart 未结束）")
+        async with runtime.lock:
+            runtime.desired = "stopped"
+            if trigger == "user":                 # 人工停止即期望态，不会自己爬起来（7.2 第 5 条）
+                runtime.heal_paused = True
+            self._touch(runtime)
+            await self._do_stop(runtime, trigger)
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def restart(self, service_id: str, trigger: str = "user") -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        self._require_manageable(runtime)
+        self._require_capability(runtime, "start")
+        self._require_capability(runtime, "stop")
+        if runtime.lock.locked():
+            raise StateConflict("生命周期操作进行中（另一 start/stop/restart 未结束）")
+        self._touch(runtime)
+        async with runtime.lock:                  # 同一把锁：restart 不做原地捷径（7.3）
+            await self._do_stop(runtime, trigger)
+            runtime.desired = "running"
+            runtime.heal_paused = False
+            runtime.heal_attempts = 0
+            runtime.next_attempt_at = 0.0
+            await self._do_start(runtime, trigger)
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def ensure(self, service_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        """幂等确保在跑（2.2）：running 直接返回，stopped 拉起并等到终态，starting 等到终态。"""
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        self._require_manageable(runtime)
+        self._require_capability(runtime, "start")
+        manifest = runtime.manifest
+        if manifest.activation == "manual":
+            raise StateConflict("activation=manual：只接受人工启动（2.2）")
+        self._touch(runtime)
+        if runtime.sm.state != "running":
+            runtime.desired = "running"
+            runtime.heal_paused = False
+            await self._start_locked(runtime, "backend")
+            deadline = time.monotonic() + manifest.health.start_grace_seconds \
+                + 2 * manifest.health.interval_seconds
+            while runtime.sm.state == "starting" and time.monotonic() < deadline:
+                await asyncio.sleep(ENSURE_POLL_SECONDS)
+        await self._persist()
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def heal(self, service_id: str, action: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        """只改纠偏意图，不碰进程（7.6）。"""
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        if action == "pause":
+            runtime.heal_paused = True
+        elif action == "resume":
+            runtime.heal_paused = False
+        elif action == "reset":
+            runtime.heal_paused = False
+            runtime.heal_attempts = 0
+            runtime.next_attempt_at = 0.0
+            runtime.last_error = None
+        else:
+            raise InvalidRequest(f"heal.action 只接受 pause|resume|reset，收到 {action!r}")
+        self._events.emit(service_id, "heal", f"自愈意图 {action}", trigger="user",
+                          context={"heal_paused": runtime.heal_paused,
+                                   "heal_attempts": runtime.heal_attempts})
+        await self._persist()
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def adopt(self, service_id: str, confirm: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        """unknown → running（7.5 第 3 条）：只认人工，且必须探测通过。"""
+        if not confirm:
+            raise StateConflict("adopt 改变归属判定，需 confirm:true（8.1）")
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        if runtime.sm.state != "unknown":
+            raise StateConflict(f"只有 unknown 可人工接管（当前 {runtime.sm.state}）")
+        pid = runtime.pid or await asyncio.to_thread(
+            port_owner_pid, runtime.manifest.listen_port)
+        if pid is None:
+            raise StateConflict("找不到在跑的进程，请用 [启动]")
+        runtime.pid = pid
+        alive = await self._health.probe(self._ctx(runtime), self._executor(runtime), runtime.health)
+        if not alive:
+            raise StateConflict(f"探测未通过，不接管：{runtime.health.last_error}")
+        runtime.started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
+        runtime.started_at_iso = get_local_iso_timestamp()
+        runtime.running_since = time.monotonic()
+        runtime.last_error = None
+        runtime.desired = "running"
+        runtime.heal_paused = False
+        runtime.heal_attempts = 0
+        runtime.sm.submit("running", f"人工接管 pid={pid}", trigger="user")
+        await self._persist()
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def terminate(self, service_id: str, confirm: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        """unknown → stopped（7.5 第 3 条）：风险动作，必须 confirm。"""
+        if not confirm:
+            raise StateConflict("terminate 会杀进程，需 confirm:true（8.1 风险动作）")
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        if runtime.sm.state != "unknown":
+            raise StateConflict(f"只有 unknown 可人工终止（当前 {runtime.sm.state}）")
+        pid = runtime.pid or await asyncio.to_thread(
+            port_owner_pid, runtime.manifest.listen_port)
+        if pid is not None:
+            await asyncio.to_thread(kill_pid_descendants, pid)
+            await asyncio.to_thread(kill_pid, pid)
+            await wait_pid_gone(pid)
+        if not await wait_port_free(runtime.manifest.listen_port):
+            raise StateConflict("端口仍被占用，终止未完成")
+        runtime.pid = None
+        runtime.desired = "stopped"
+        runtime.heal_paused = True
+        runtime.sm.submit("stopped", "人工终止确认", trigger="user")
+        await self._persist()
+        return self.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+    async def run_action(self, service_id: str, action_id: str,
+                         confirm: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        self._require_manageable(runtime)
+        manifest = runtime.manifest
+        action = manifest.action(action_id)
+        if action is None or not manifest.capabilities.actions:
+            raise CapabilityNotDeclared(f"未声明动作: {action_id}（8.4 白名单，403）")
+        if action.requires_running and runtime.sm.state != "running":
+            raise StateConflict(
+                f"动作 {action_id} 需要服务在运行（当前 {runtime.sm.state}）")
+        if action.confirm and not confirm:
+            raise StateConflict(f"动作 {action_id} 声明了 confirm:true，需显式确认")
+        self._touch(runtime)
+        task = await self._actions.run(self._ctx(runtime), action)
+        self._events.emit(service_id, "action", f"动作 {action_id} {task['state']}",
+                          trigger="action",
+                          context={"action_id": action_id, "task_id": task["task_id"],
+                                   "exit_code": task.get("exit_code")})
+        await self._persist()
+        return task
+
+    def action_task(self, task_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        return self._actions.task(task_id)
+
+    async def log_tail(self, service_id: str, tail: int = 200) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        runtime = self._require(service_id)
+        logs = runtime.manifest.logs
+        if logs.kind != "stdout":
+            return {"path": None, "lines": [],
+                    "note": "logs.kind 非 stdout：日志由服务自写（6.1 T1）"}
+        path = paths.service_data_dir(runtime.manifest.id) / logs.path
+        lines = await asyncio.to_thread(
+            _read_tail, path, tail, runtime.manifest.spec.encoding)
+        return {"path": str(path), "lines": lines}
+
+    # ── 状态快照（7.4 一套字段两个视图）────────────────────────
+    def snapshot_all(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        payload: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
+        for service_id, item in self._registry.items.items():
+            runtime = self._runtimes.get(service_id)
+            payload.append(self._invalid_snapshot(item) if runtime is None
+                           else self._snapshot(runtime))
+        payload.sort(key=lambda entry: entry["name"])      # Tab 与瓦片永不重排（4.6）
+        return payload
+
+    def snapshot_one(self, service_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        return self._snapshot(self._require(service_id), detail=True)
+
+    def events(self, service_id: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        return self._events.tail(service_id, limit)
+
+    # ── tick：探测 → 判态 → 纠偏 ─────────────────────────────
+    async def _tick_loop(self) -> None:
+        while True:
+            try:
+                await asyncio.sleep(TICK_SECONDS)
+                await self._tick()
+            except asyncio.CancelledError:
+                raise
+            except Exception:
+                logger.exception("[svc] tick 异常（已隔离，循环继续）")
+
+    async def _tick(self) -> None:
+        for runtime in list(self._runtimes.values()):
+            try:
+                await self._sync_presence(runtime)
+                if runtime.manifest.kind in MONITOR_KINDS:
+                    await self._tick_monitor(runtime)
+                else:
+                    await self._tick_service(runtime)
+                await self._reconcile(runtime)
+            except Exception:                 # 8.8：一个服务坏了不能冻结全站
+                logger.exception(f"[svc] {runtime.manifest.id} tick 异常（已隔离）")
+
+    async def _tick_service(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        if state not in ("starting", "running"):
+            return
+        now = time.monotonic()
+        if now < runtime.next_probe_at:
+            return
+        runtime.next_probe_at = now + runtime.manifest.health.interval_seconds
+        executor = self._executor(runtime)
+        health = runtime.manifest.health
+        if state == "starting":
+            if not self._grace_elapsed(runtime):
+                return
+            if await self._health.probe(self._ctx(runtime), executor, runtime.health):
+                runtime.running_since = time.monotonic()
+                runtime.last_error = None
+                runtime.sm.submit("running", "健康探测通过", trigger="self-heal")
+                await self._persist()
+                return
+            if self._start_timed_out(runtime) or runtime.health.consecutive_fail >= health.retries:
+                await self._kill_runtime_process(runtime)     # 先树杀再判 failed（7.1）
+                runtime.last_error = runtime.health.last_error or "启动未就绪"
+                runtime.sm.submit("failed", f"启动未就绪（{runtime.last_error}）",
+                                  trigger="self-heal")
+                self._schedule_backoff(runtime)
+                await self._persist()
+            return
+        if await self._health.probe(self._ctx(runtime), executor, runtime.health):
+            if (runtime.running_since is not None and runtime.heal_attempts
+                    and time.monotonic() - runtime.running_since >= HEALTHY_RESET_SECONDS):
+                runtime.heal_attempts = 0
+                await self._persist()
+            return
+        if runtime.health.consecutive_fail >= health.retries:
+            await self._kill_runtime_process(runtime)
+            runtime.last_error = runtime.health.last_error or "健康连续失败"
+            runtime.sm.submit("failed", f"健康连续失败 {runtime.health.consecutive_fail} 次",
+                              trigger="self-heal")
+            self._schedule_backoff(runtime)
+            await self._persist()
+
+    async def _tick_monitor(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        """纯监控型：没有进程可拉，用 started→running 两步把"探到了"变成合法迁移（7.1）。"""
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        if state in ("invalid", "disabled"):
+            return
+        now = time.monotonic()
+        if now < runtime.next_probe_at:
+            return
+        runtime.next_probe_at = now + runtime.manifest.health.interval_seconds
+        if state in ("stopped", "failed"):
+            runtime.sm.submit("starting", "接入监控（kind 不拥有进程）", trigger="self-heal")
+        if runtime.sm.state != "starting":
+            return
+        if await self._health.probe(self._ctx(runtime), self._executor(runtime), runtime.health):
+            runtime.started_monotonic = runtime.started_monotonic or time.monotonic()
+            runtime.running_since = time.monotonic()
+            runtime.sm.submit("running", "外部端点探测通过", trigger="self-heal")
+        elif runtime.health.consecutive_fail >= runtime.manifest.health.retries:
+            runtime.sm.submit("failed", runtime.health.last_error or "外部端点不可达",
+                              trigger="self-heal")
+
+    async def _sync_presence(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        """presence 变化时刷新 <OMNI_SERVICE_DATA>/presence（6.7 第 5 条）。
+        只在服务活着时写有意义：服务已停就没人读它，写了只会让下次启动先读到旧值。
+        """
+        if runtime.sm.state not in ("starting", "running"):
+            return
+        presence = self._presence()
+        if presence == runtime.presence_written:
+            return
+        runtime.presence_written = presence
+        await asyncio.to_thread(
+            write_presence, paths.service_data_dir(runtime.manifest.id), presence)
+
+    async def _reconcile(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        manifest = runtime.manifest
+        if manifest.kind in MONITOR_KINDS:
+            return                                     # 纯监控型没有启动动作，恢复靠下一次探测
+        if not manifest.enabled or manifest.activation == "manual":
+            return                                     # manual 只认人手（7.6 三不越界）
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        if state in ("unknown", "starting", "stopping", "invalid", "disabled"):
+            return                                     # 不猜 unknown，不打断中途态
+        if runtime.desired == "running" and not runtime.heal_paused:
+            if state in ("stopped", "failed") and manifest.restart.policy != "never":
+                if runtime.heal_attempts >= self._max_attempts(runtime):
+                    self._stop_healing(runtime, "已到重试上限，自愈已暂停")
+                    return
+                if time.monotonic() < runtime.next_attempt_at:
+                    return
+                if self._presence() == "present" and self._inflight() > 0:
+                    return                             # 在场不打断在飞请求，推迟到下一 tick
+                runtime.heal_attempts += 1
+                self._events.emit(manifest.id, "heal",
+                                  f"纠偏启动（第 {runtime.heal_attempts} 次）",
+                                  trigger="self-heal",
+                                  context={"attempt": runtime.heal_attempts,
+                                           "presence": self._presence()})
+                try:
+                    await self._start_locked(runtime, "self-heal")
+                except StateConflict as exc:
+                    runtime.next_attempt_at = time.monotonic() + TICK_SECONDS
+                    logger.debug(f"[svc] {manifest.id} 纠偏推迟: {exc}")
+                return
+        if runtime.desired == "stopped" and state in ("running", "failed"):
+            await self._stop_tolerant(runtime, "self-heal")    # 停止同样是纠偏（7.6）
+            return
+        if (manifest.activation == "on_demand" and manifest.idle_stop_seconds > 0
+                and state == "running" and self._idle_expired(runtime)):
+            runtime.desired = "stopped"
+            self._events.emit(manifest.id, "idle_stop",
+                              f"空闲 {manifest.idle_stop_seconds}s 自动停止",
+                              trigger="self-heal",
+                              context={"last_active_at": runtime.last_active_at})
+            await self._stop_tolerant(runtime, "self-heal")
+
+    # ── 启动 / 停止的实际动作 ────────────────────────────────
+    async def _start_locked(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, trigger: str) -> None:
+        if runtime.lock.locked():
+            if runtime.sm.state == "starting":
+                return                                 # 已受理，幂等（7.2 第 1 条）
+            raise StateConflict("生命周期操作进行中（另一 start/stop/restart 未结束）")
+        async with runtime.lock:
+            await self._do_start(runtime, trigger)
+
+    async def _stop_tolerant(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, trigger: str) -> None:
+        if runtime.lock.locked():
+            return                                     # 抢不过就不抢，下一 tick 再来
+        async with runtime.lock:
+            await self._do_stop(runtime, trigger)
+
+    async def _do_start(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, trigger: str) -> None:
+        manifest = runtime.manifest
+        if not manifest.enabled:
+            raise StateConflict(f"{manifest.id} 清单 enabled=false，操作已禁用")
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        if state in ("running", "starting"):
+            return
+        if state not in ("stopped", "failed"):
+            raise StateConflict(f"当前状态 {state} 不可启动（7.1）")
+        port = manifest.listen_port
+        if port and await asyncio.to_thread(port_listening, port):
+            owner = await asyncio.to_thread(port_owner_pid, port)
+            runtime.last_error = f"端口 {port} 已被占用（PID {owner}）"
+            runtime.sm.submit("failed", runtime.last_error, trigger=trigger,
+                              context={"port": port, "owner_pid": owner})
+            await self._persist()
+            raise StateConflict(runtime.last_error)
+        runtime.started_monotonic = None
+        runtime.running_since = None
+        runtime.next_probe_at = 0.0
+        if not runtime.sm.submit("starting", "启动受理", trigger=trigger):
+            return
+        runtime.started_monotonic = time.monotonic()
+        runtime.started_at_iso = get_local_iso_timestamp()
+        presence = self._presence()
+        runtime.presence_written = presence        # 6.7 第 5 条：进程读到的是启动初值
+        await asyncio.to_thread(write_presence, paths.service_data_dir(manifest.id), presence)
+        result = await self._executor(runtime).start(self._ctx(runtime))
+        if not result.ok:
+            runtime.pid = None
+            runtime.last_error = result.error or "启动失败"
+            runtime.sm.submit("failed", runtime.last_error, trigger=trigger)
+            self._schedule_backoff(runtime)
+            await self._persist()
+            return
+        runtime.pid = result.pid
+        logger.info(f"[svc] {manifest.id} 启动受理完成 pid={result.pid}（等健康确认才转 running）")
+        await self._persist()
+
+    async def _do_stop(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, trigger: str) -> None:
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        if state == "stopped":
+            runtime.pid = None
+            await self._persist()
+            return
+        if not runtime.sm.submit("stopping", "停止受理", trigger=trigger):
+            return
+        result = await self._executor(runtime).stop(self._ctx(runtime))
+        if result.pid_gone and result.port_free:
+            runtime.pid = None
+            runtime.started_monotonic = None
+            runtime.running_since = None
+            runtime.sm.submit("stopped", "树杀完成且端口释放", trigger=trigger)
+        else:
+            runtime.last_error = result.error or (
+                f"停止未确认 pid_gone={result.pid_gone} port_free={result.port_free}")
+            runtime.sm.submit("failed", runtime.last_error, trigger=trigger,
+                              context={"pid_gone": result.pid_gone,
+                                       "port_free": result.port_free})
+        await self._persist()
+
+    async def _kill_runtime_process(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        """转 failed 前先树杀：不杀会留僵尸把下次启动堵成"端口占用"（7.1 / 8.2 第 6 步）。"""
+        try:
+            await self._executor(runtime).stop(self._ctx(runtime))
+        except Exception:
+            logger.exception(f"[svc] {runtime.manifest.id} 转 failed 前树杀异常（继续迁移）")
+        runtime.pid = None
+        runtime.started_monotonic = None
+
+    # ── 收养与快照落盘 ───────────────────────────────────────
+    async def _restore_from_snapshot(self) -> None:
+        snapshot = await asyncio.to_thread(self._runtime_store.load)
+        for service_id, runtime in self._runtimes.items():
+            record = snapshot.get(service_id) or {}
+            runtime.desired = "running" if record.get("desired") == "running" else "stopped"
+            runtime.heal_paused = bool(record.get("heal_paused"))
+            runtime.heal_attempts = int(record.get("heal_attempts") or 0)
+            runtime.last_active_at = record.get("last_active_at")
+            pid = record.get("pid")
+            if not isinstance(pid, int):
+                port = runtime.manifest.listen_port
+                if port and await asyncio.to_thread(port_listening, port):
+                    runtime.sm.force_initial("unknown")        # 7.5 第 3 条：不猜，交人工
+                    runtime.last_error = "运行时快照丢失但端口在监听"
+                continue
+            if not await asyncio.to_thread(pid_alive, pid):
+                continue                                       # 进程已死：交给纠偏
+            if not await self._cmdline_matches(pid, runtime.manifest):
+                logger.warning(
+                    f"[svc] {service_id} 快照 PID {pid} 命令行与清单不符，不收养（7.5 第 2 条）")
+                continue
+            runtime.pid = pid
+            runtime.started_monotonic = time.monotonic() - float(record.get("uptime_s") or 0)
+            runtime.started_at_iso = record.get("started_at")
+            alive = await self._health.probe(
+                self._ctx(runtime), self._executor(runtime), runtime.health)
+            runtime.sm.force_initial("running" if alive else "unknown")
+            if alive:
+                runtime.running_since = time.monotonic()
+            logger.info(f"[svc] {service_id} 孤儿收养 pid={pid} running={alive}")
+
+    async def _cmdline_matches(self, pid: int, manifest: ServiceManifest) -> bool:
+        expected = getattr(manifest.spec, "command", "")
+        if not expected:
+            return False
+        try:
+            cmdline = await asyncio.to_thread(lambda: psutil.Process(pid).cmdline())
+        except psutil.Error as exc:
+            logger.debug(f"[svc] 读取 pid={pid} 命令行失败: {exc}")
+            return False
+        return Path(expected).name.lower() in " ".join(cmdline).lower()
+
+    async def _persist(self) -> None:
+        payload: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
+        for service_id, runtime in self._runtimes.items():
+            payload[service_id] = {
+                "pid": runtime.pid,
+                "desired": runtime.desired,
+                "heal_paused": runtime.heal_paused,
+                "heal_attempts": runtime.heal_attempts,
+                "last_active_at": runtime.last_active_at,
+                "started_at": runtime.started_at_iso,
+                "uptime_s": int(time.monotonic() - runtime.started_monotonic)
+                if runtime.started_monotonic else 0,
+            }
+        await asyncio.to_thread(self._runtime_store.save, payload)
+
+    # ── 小工具 ──────────────────────────────────────────────
+    def _new_runtime(self, item: RegistryItem) -> ServiceRuntime:
+        manifest = item.manifest
+        if manifest is None:
+            raise InvalidRequest(f"{item.service_id} 清单非法，不能建运行态")
+        runtime = ServiceRuntime(
+            manifest=manifest,
+            service_dir=item.service_dir,
+            sm=StateMachine(manifest.id, on_change=self._on_state_change),
+        )
+        if not manifest.enabled:
+            runtime.sm.submit("disabled", "清单 enabled=false", trigger="scan")
+        elif manifest.kind in MONITOR_KINDS:
+            runtime.desired = "running"      # 纯监控型无启动动作，期望态恒为"应可达"（8.7）
+        return runtime
+
+    def _on_state_change(self, service_id: str, from_state: str, to_state: str,
+                         reason: str, trigger: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
+        self._events.emit(service_id, "transition", reason, from_state=from_state,
+                          to_state=to_state, trigger=trigger, context=context)
+
+    def _require(self, service_id: str) -> ServiceRuntime:
+        runtime = self._runtimes.get(service_id)
+        if runtime is not None:
+            return runtime
+        item = self._registry.get(service_id)
+        if item is not None and item.invalid:
+            raise InvalidRequest(f"{service_id} 清单非法：{item.error}")
+        raise ServiceNotFound(f"服务不存在: {service_id}")
+
+    def _require_manageable(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        if state == "invalid":
+            raise InvalidRequest(f"{runtime.manifest.id} 清单非法，修正 service.json 后刷新")
+        if not runtime.manifest.enabled or state == "disabled":
+            raise StateConflict(f"{runtime.manifest.id} 清单 enabled=false，操作已禁用")
+
+    def _require_capability(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, name: str) -> None:
+        """能力闸门（8.7）：kind=http-endpoint 无 start/stop，API 也不能绕过按钮直接调。"""
+        if not getattr(runtime.manifest.capabilities, name):
+            raise StateConflict(
+                f"{runtime.manifest.id} kind={runtime.manifest.kind} 未开放 {name} 能力（8.7）")
+
+    def _executor(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> Executor:
+        executor = self._executors.get(runtime.manifest.kind)
+        if executor is None:
+            raise InvalidRequest(f"kind={runtime.manifest.kind} 无执行器（8.7）")
+        return executor
+
+    def _ctx(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> ServiceContext:
+        return ServiceContext(
+            manifest=runtime.manifest,
+            service_dir=runtime.service_dir,
+            data_dir=paths.service_data_dir(runtime.manifest.id),
+            pid=runtime.pid,
+            presence=self._presence(),
+        )
+
+    def _presence(self) -> str:
+        if self._presence_provider is None:
+            return "away"
+        try:
+            return "present" if self._presence_provider() == "present" else "away"
+        except Exception:
+            return "away"
+
+    def _inflight(self) -> int:
+        if self._inflight_provider is None:
+            return 0
+        try:
+            return int(self._inflight_provider())
+        except Exception:
+            return 0
+
+    def _touch(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        runtime.last_active_at = get_local_iso_timestamp()
+
+    def _grace_elapsed(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> bool:
+        started = runtime.started_monotonic
+        return started is not None and \
+            time.monotonic() - started >= runtime.manifest.health.start_grace_seconds
+
+    def _start_timed_out(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> bool:
+        started = runtime.started_monotonic
+        if started is None:
+            return False
+        health = runtime.manifest.health
+        return time.monotonic() - started > health.start_grace_seconds + 2 * health.interval_seconds
+
+    def _max_attempts(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> int:
+        attempts = runtime.manifest.restart.max_attempts
+        if self._presence() == "present":              # 在场更保守（7.6 表）
+            return max(1, attempts - 1)
+        return attempts
+
+    def _schedule_backoff(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> None:
+        backoff = runtime.manifest.restart.backoff_seconds
+        index = min(max(runtime.heal_attempts - 1, 0), len(backoff) - 1)
+        delay = backoff[index] * (2 if self._presence() == "present" else 1)
+        runtime.next_attempt_at = time.monotonic() + delay
+
+    def _stop_healing(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, reason: str) -> None:
+        if runtime.heal_paused and runtime.sm.state == "failed":
+            return                                     # 已止损，不重复记事件
+        runtime.heal_paused = True
+        runtime.last_error = reason
+        self._events.emit(runtime.manifest.id, "heal", reason, trigger="self-heal",
+                          context={"attempt": runtime.heal_attempts, "result": "crashloop"})
+        logger.warning(f"[svc] {runtime.manifest.id} 止损: {reason}"
+                       f"（已试 {runtime.heal_attempts} 次）")
+
+    def _idle_expired(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> bool:
+        if not runtime.last_active_at:
+            return False
+        try:
+            last_active = datetime.fromisoformat(runtime.last_active_at)
+        except ValueError:
+            return False
+        return (datetime.now() - last_active).total_seconds() > runtime.manifest.idle_stop_seconds
+
+    def _uptime(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime) -> int:
+        if runtime.sm.state != "running" or runtime.started_monotonic is None:
+            return 0
+        return max(0, int(time.monotonic() - runtime.started_monotonic))
+
+    def _item_error(self, service_id: str) -> Optional[str]:
+        item = self._registry.get(service_id)
+        return item.error if item is not None else None
+
+    def _snapshot(self, runtime: ServiceRuntime, detail: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        manifest = runtime.manifest
+        state = runtime.sm.state
+        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
+            "id": manifest.id,
+            "name": manifest.name,
+            "kind": manifest.kind,
+            "state": state,
+            "desired": runtime.desired,
+            "heal_paused": runtime.heal_paused,
+            "heal_attempts": runtime.heal_attempts,
+            "last_active_at": runtime.last_active_at,
+            "alive": state == "running",
+            "port": manifest.listen_port,
+            "uptime_s": self._uptime(runtime),
+            "capabilities": manifest.capabilities.model_dump(),
+            "actions": [action.model_dump(exclude_none=True) for action in manifest.actions]
+            if manifest.capabilities.actions else [],
+            "config_dirty": runtime.config_dirty or manifest.id in self._registry.dirty_ids,
+            "last_error": runtime.last_error,
+            "error": self._item_error(manifest.id),
+        }
+        if detail:
+            payload["detail"] = {
+                "pid": runtime.pid,
+                "health": runtime.health.as_detail(),
+                "health_history": runtime.health.history_list(),
+            }
+        return payload
+
+    def _invalid_snapshot(self, item: RegistryItem) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        return {
+            "id": item.service_id,
+            "name": item.service_id,
+            "kind": "unknown",
+            "state": "invalid",
+            "desired": "stopped",
+            "heal_paused": True,
+            "heal_attempts": 0,
+            "last_active_at": None,
+            "alive": False,
+            "port": None,
+            "uptime_s": 0,
+            "capabilities": {"start": False, "stop": False, "status": False,
+                             "logs": False, "actions": False},
+            "actions": [],
+            "config_dirty": False,
+            "last_error": None,
+            "error": item.error,
+        }
+
+
+def _read_tail(path: Path, tail: int, encoding: str) -> List[str]:
+    """日志尾部：只读末 LOG_TAIL_BYTES，按清单声明的编码解码（6.2.5）。"""
+    try:
+        with path.open("rb") as handle:
+            handle.seek(0, 2)
+            size = handle.tell()
+            handle.seek(max(0, size - LOG_TAIL_BYTES))
+            blob = handle.read()
+    except FileNotFoundError:
+        return []
+    except OSError as exc:
+        logger.warning(f"[svc] 日志读取失败 {path}: {exc}")
+        return []
+    return blob.decode(encoding, errors="replace").splitlines()[-tail:]
```

#### 8.10.19 `support_services/__init__.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/support_services/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,24 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""support_services — 支撑服务微型运维框架（L1）包入口 — 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+单例延迟导入：任何子模块 import 本包时不能触发 manager 装配（否则 registry/manifest
+与本包形成导入环）。manager 只在 get_manager() 首次调用时构造。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Optional
+
+if TYPE_CHECKING:                        # 仅供类型检查，运行期不导入
+    from app.support_services.manager import ServiceManager
+
+_manager: Optional["ServiceManager"] = None
+
+
+def get_manager() -> "ServiceManager":
+    """进程内单例：健康/纠偏循环只有一份，路由与 startup 共用。"""
+    global _manager
+    if _manager is None:
+        from app.support_services.manager import ServiceManager
+
+        _manager = ServiceManager()
+    return _manager
```

#### 8.10.20 `api/v1/service_routes.py`

```diff
--- /dev/null
+++ b/backend/app/api/v1/service_routes.py
@@ -0,0 +1,145 @@
+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""service_routes — 支撑服务管理 API 路由薄壳（8.1）— 小欧 2026-11-13
+
+包装与 fs_routes 同体例：只解包参数、只转译异常，不写业务、不碰 DB。
+挂载走 main.py 的 _mount()（唯一入口，自动 token 鉴权），无新增豁免。
+
+端点 12 个：
+- GET    /services                          全量快照（瓦片区与 Tab label 同源）
+- GET    /services/{id}                     单服务详情（含健康历史尾部）
+- POST   /services/{id}/start|stop|restart   幂等受理，不等待终态
+- POST   /services/{id}/ensure              幂等确保在跑（Agent / 任务前置）
+- POST   /services/{id}/heal                自愈意图 pause|resume|reset
+- POST   /services/{id}/adopt|terminate     人工裁决（confirm:true）
+- POST   /services/{id}/actions/{action_id} 动作执行（短动作同步返回，长动作返回 task_id）
+- GET    /services/{id}/logs?tail=N         日志尾部
+- GET    /action-tasks/{task_id}            长动作进度
+
+状态码只有 ServiceError.status 一档来源（8.1：403 / 404 / 409 / 422），本层不自造判定。
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import functools
+from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Dict, List, Optional
+
+from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
+from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
+
+from app.support_services import get_manager
+from app.support_services.errors import ServiceError
+from app.support_services.manager import ServiceManager
+
+router = APIRouter()
+
+
+class HealRequest(BaseModel):
+    action: str = Field(..., pattern="^(pause|resume|reset)$")
+
+
+class ConfirmRequest(BaseModel):
+    """adopt / terminate / 附加动作共用的裁决体：缺省 false，漏传必然 409（8.1 风险动作）。"""
+
+    confirm: bool = False
+
+
+def _manager() -> ServiceManager:
+    """依赖注入取单例：测试用 dependency_overrides 替换，不动路由代码。"""
+    return get_manager()
+
+
+def _guarded(handler: Callable[..., Awaitable[Any]]) -> Callable[..., Awaitable[Any]]:
+    """异常一次转译（8.1）：ServiceError.status 就是前端报因用的四档状态码。"""
+
+    @functools.wraps(handler)
+    async def wrapper(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
+        try:
+            return await handler(*args, **kwargs)
+        except ServiceError as exc:
+            raise HTTPException(status_code=exc.status, detail=exc.message) from exc
+
+    return wrapper
+
+
+@router.get("/services")
+@_guarded
+async def list_services(manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    """全量快照一次带齐（8.1）：前端不得再逐个拉详情凑字段，也不得自算 uptime。"""
+    return manager.snapshot_all()
+
+
+@router.get("/services/{service_id}")
+@_guarded
+async def get_service_detail(service_id: str,
+                      manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return manager.snapshot_one(service_id)
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/start")
+@_guarded
+async def start_service(service_id: str,
+                        manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.start(service_id, trigger="user")
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/stop")
+@_guarded
+async def stop_service(service_id: str,
+                       manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.stop(service_id, trigger="user")
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/restart")
+@_guarded
+async def restart_service(service_id: str,
+                          manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.restart(service_id, trigger="user")
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/ensure")
+@_guarded
+async def ensure_service(service_id: str,
+                         manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.ensure(service_id)
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/heal")
+@_guarded
+async def heal_service(service_id: str, body: HealRequest,
+                       manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.heal(service_id, body.action)
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/adopt")
+@_guarded
+async def adopt_service(service_id: str, body: ConfirmRequest,
+                        manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.adopt(service_id, body.confirm)
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/terminate")
+@_guarded
+async def terminate_service(service_id: str, body: ConfirmRequest,
+                            manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.terminate(service_id, body.confirm)
+
+
+@router.post("/services/{service_id}/actions/{action_id}")
+@_guarded
+async def run_action(service_id: str, action_id: str,
+                     body: Optional[ConfirmRequest] = None,
+                     manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.run_action(service_id, action_id, bool(body and body.confirm))
+
+
+@router.get("/services/{service_id}/logs")
+@_guarded
+async def service_logs(service_id: str, tail: int = Query(200, ge=1, le=2000),
+                       manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return await manager.log_tail(service_id, tail)
+
+
+@router.get("/action-tasks/{task_id}")
+@_guarded
+async def get_action_task(task_id: str,
+                      manager: ServiceManager = Depends(_manager)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return manager.action_task(task_id)
```
